using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

[ApiController]
[Route("api/authors")]
public class AuthorsController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly AppDbContext _db;
    public AuthorsController(AppDbContext db) => _db = db;

    [HttpGet("{id:guid}")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Get(Guid id, CancellationToken ct)
    {
        var data = await _db.Authors.AsNoTracking()
            .Where(a => a.Id == id)
            .Select(a => new
            {
                a.Id,
                a.Name,
                LastModified = EF.Property<DateTimeOffset>(a, "LastModified")
            })
            .SingleOrDefaultAsync(ct);

        if (data is null) return NotFound();

        Response.Headers.LastModified = data.LastModified.UtcDateTime.ToString("R");

        // Basic If-Modified-Since check (seconds precision)
        if (Request.Headers.IfModifiedSince.Count > 0 &&
            DateTimeOffset.TryParse(Request.Headers.IfModifiedSince.ToString(), out var ims))
        {
            var lmSeconds = new DateTimeOffset(
                data.LastModified.UtcDateTime.AddTicks(-(data.LastModified.UtcDateTime.Ticks % TimeSpan.TicksPerSecond)),
                TimeSpan.Zero);

            if (lmSeconds <= ims.ToUniversalTime())
                return StatusCode(StatusCodes.Status304NotModified);
        }

        return Ok(new { data.Id, data.Name });
    }
} ^eFC614Gc

using Polly;
using Polly.Retry;

builder.Services.AddResiliencePipeline<string, HttpResponseMessage>("catalog-pipeline", b =>
{
    b.AddRetry(new RetryStrategyOptions<HttpResponseMessage>
    {
        MaxRetryAttempts = 3,
        UseJitter = true,
        BackoffType = DelayBackoffType.Exponential,
        Delay = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(200),

        DelayGenerator = args =>
        {
            // args.Outcome.Result is HttpResponseMessage when it's a result-based retry
            var resp = args.Outcome.Result;
            if (resp?.Headers.RetryAfter is { } ra)
            {
                TimeSpan delay =
                    ra.Delta ?? (ra.Date.HasValue ? ra.Date.Value - DateTimeOffset.UtcNow : TimeSpan.Zero);

                delay = delay < TimeSpan.Zero ? TimeSpan.Zero : delay;
                if (delay > TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30)) delay = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30);

                return new ValueTask<TimeSpan?>(delay);
            }

            // return null to use the built-in exponential+jitter calculation
            return new ValueTask<TimeSpan?>((TimeSpan?)null);
        },

        ShouldHandle = args =>
            ValueTask.FromResult(respShouldRetry(args.Outcome))
    });
});

static bool respShouldRetry(Outcome<HttpResponseMessage> outcome)
{
    if (outcome.Exception is HttpRequestException) return true;

    var r = outcome.Result;
    if (r is null) return false;

    return r.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.TooManyRequests ||
           r.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.ServiceUnavailable ||
           (int)r.StatusCode >= 500;
} ^yKK8JPtX

using System.Net;
using Polly;
using Polly.Extensions.Http;

static TimeSpan ComputeDelay(HttpResponseMessage response, int attempt)
{
    // 1) Use Retry-After if present
    if (response.Headers.RetryAfter is { } ra)
    {
        if (ra.Delta is TimeSpan delta)
            return Clamp(delta);

        if (ra.Date is DateTimeOffset date)
            return Clamp(date - DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);
    }

    // 2) Otherwise use exponential backoff + jitter
    var baseDelay = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(200 * Math.Pow(2, attempt - 1)); // 200ms, 400ms, 800ms...
    var jitter = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(Random.Shared.Next(0, 200));
    return Clamp(baseDelay + jitter);
}

static TimeSpan Clamp(TimeSpan delay)
{
    if (delay < TimeSpan.Zero) return TimeSpan.Zero;
    // Protect yourself from crazy Retry-After values
    var max = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30);
    return delay > max ? max : delay;
}

static IAsyncPolicy<HttpResponseMessage> RetryWithRetryAfterPolicy() =>
    Policy<HttpResponseMessage>
        .HandleResult(r => r.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.TooManyRequests ||
                           r.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.ServiceUnavailable ||
                           (int)r.StatusCode >= 500) // optional
        .WaitAndRetryAsync(
            retryCount: 3,
            sleepDurationProvider: (outcome, attempt, context) =>
                ComputeDelay(outcome.Result, attempt),
            onRetry: (outcome, delay, attempt, context) =>
            {
                // outcome.Result has status + headers
                // log delay/attempt if you want
            }); ^iH9ulF6I
